{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_01UenDemKvahEBDp5BNFLiEA","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_017f1LBJHo2kGfhttT6hMrkK","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/normalize_defects.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nManufacturing Defect Reason Normalization Tool\n\nThis script normalizes hand-written defect reasons from test center logs\ninto standardized codebook format, handling typos, mixed languages,\nabbreviations, and other inconsistencies.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport csv\nimport json\nimport re\nfrom typing import List, Dict, Tuple, Optional\nfrom difflib import SequenceMatcher\nimport unicodedata\n\n\nclass DefectNormalizer:\n    \"\"\"Normalizes manufacturing defect reasons to standard codebook format.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, codebook_dir: str = \"/app/data\"):\n        self.codebook_dir = codebook_dir\n        self.codebooks = {}\n        self.load_codebooks()\n\n    def load_codebooks(self):\n        \"\"\"Load all product codebooks into memory.\"\"\"\n        products = [\"P1_POWER\", \"P2_CTRL\", \"P3_RF\"]\n        for product in products:\n            codebook_path = f\"{self.codebook_dir}/codebook_{product}.csv\"\n            self.codebooks[product] = []\n\n            with open(codebook_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n                reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n                for row in reader:\n                    # Parse station_scope into a list\n                    stations = [s.strip() for s in row['station_scope'].split(';')]\n                    # Parse keywords into a list\n                    keywords = [k.strip() for k in row['keywords_examples'].split(',')]\n\n                    self.codebooks[product].append({\n                        'code': row['code'],\n                        'label': row['standard_label'],\n                        'category_lv1': row['category_lv1'],\n                        'category_lv2': row['category_lv2'],\n                        'stations': stations,\n                        'keywords': keywords\n                    })\n\n    def normalize_text(self, text: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Normalize text for comparison (lowercase, remove extra spaces).\"\"\"\n        # Normalize unicode characters\n        text = unicodedata.normalize('NFKC', text)\n        # Convert to lowercase\n        text = text.lower()\n        # Remove extra whitespace\n        text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text).strip()\n        return text\n\n    def segment_raw_text(self, raw_text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Segment raw text into meaningful parts for matching.\n        Handles mixed Chinese-English, separators, and multiple issues.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Common separators used by engineers\n        separators = [',', '，', '&', '/', ';', '；', ' and ', ' then ', ' 重新 ']\n\n        # Replace all separators with a standard one\n        text = raw_text\n        for sep in separators:\n            text = text.replace(sep, '|||')\n\n        # Split by the standard separator\n        segments = [s.strip() for s in text.split('|||') if s.strip()]\n\n        # If no segments found, return the whole text as one segment\n        if not segments:\n            segments = [raw_text.strip()]\n\n        return segments\n\n    def extract_net_names(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Extract signal/net names from text (e.g., I2C_SDA, SW_NODE, R152_NET).\"\"\"\n        # Pattern for net names: uppercase letters, numbers, underscores\n        pattern = r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{2,}\\b'\n        return re.findall(pattern, text)\n\n    def extract_component_names(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Extract component references (e.g., U202, R152, C87, Q15).\"\"\"\n        # Pattern for component names: letter(s) followed by numbers\n        pattern = r'\\b[A-Z]+\\d+\\b'\n        return re.findall(pattern, text)\n\n    def keyword_match_score(self, segment: str, keywords: List[str]) -> float:\n        \"\"\"\n        Calculate keyword match score between segment and codebook keywords.\n        Returns a score between 0.0 and 1.0.\n        \"\"\"\n        segment_norm = self.normalize_text(segment)\n        matches = 0\n        total_keywords = len(keywords)\n\n        if total_keywords == 0:\n            return 0.0\n\n        for keyword in keywords:\n            keyword_norm = self.normalize_text(keyword)\n            # Exact match\n            if keyword_norm in segment_norm:\n                matches += 1\n            # Partial match with high similarity\n            elif self.string_similarity(keyword_norm, segment_norm) > 0.8:\n                matches += 0.5\n\n        return min(matches / total_keywords, 1.0)\n\n    def string_similarity(self, s1: str, s2: str) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate string similarity using SequenceMatcher.\"\"\"\n        return SequenceMatcher(None, s1, s2).ratio()\n\n    def find_best_match(self, segment: str, product_id: str, station: str) -> Tuple[Optional[Dict], float]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Find the best matching codebook entry for a segment.\n        Returns (codebook_entry, confidence_score).\n        \"\"\"\n        codebook = self.codebooks.get(product_id, [])\n        if not codebook:\n            return None, 0.0\n\n        segment_norm = self.normalize_text(segment)\n        net_names = self.extract_net_names(segment)\n        component_names = self.extract_component_names(segment)\n\n        best_match = None\n        best_score = 0.0\n\n        for entry in codebook:\n            score = 0.0\n\n            # Keyword matching (most important)\n            keyword_score = self.keyword_match_score(segment, entry['keywords'])\n            score += keyword_score * 0.6\n\n            # Net name matching\n            entry_label_norm = self.normalize_text(entry['label'])\n            for net in net_names:\n                if net.lower() in entry_label_norm:\n                    score += 0.15\n                    break\n\n            # Component name matching\n            for comp in component_names:\n                if comp.lower() in entry_label_norm:\n                    score += 0.15\n                    break\n\n            # Station validation (important for correctness)\n            if station in entry['stations']:\n                score += 0.1\n            else:\n                # Penalize if station doesn't match\n                score *= 0.5\n\n            if score > best_score:\n                best_score = score\n                best_match = entry\n\n        return best_match, best_score\n\n    def calibrate_confidence(self, match_score: float, segment: str, entry: Optional[Dict]) -> float:\n        \"\"\"\n        Calibrate confidence score based on match quality and engineering requirements.\n        Uses a more conservative approach for low-quality matches.\n        \"\"\"\n        if entry is None:\n            return 0.0\n\n        # Base confidence from match score\n        confidence = match_score\n\n        # Apply non-linear calibration to spread out confidence values\n        # This helps engineers validate using means, quantiles, and diversity\n        if confidence >= 0.7:\n            # High confidence: keep high\n            confidence = 0.75 + (confidence - 0.7) * 0.833  # Maps 0.7-1.0 to 0.75-1.0\n        elif confidence >= 0.5:\n            # Medium confidence: moderate\n            confidence = 0.5 + (confidence - 0.5) * 1.25  # Maps 0.5-0.7 to 0.5-0.75\n        elif confidence >= 0.3:\n            # Low confidence: penalize more\n            confidence = 0.25 + (confidence - 0.3) * 1.25  # Maps 0.3-0.5 to 0.25-0.5\n        else:\n            # Very low confidence: mark as unknown\n            confidence = confidence * 0.8  # Maps 0-0.3 to 0-0.24\n\n        return round(confidence, 3)\n\n    def normalize_record(self, record: Dict) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"\n        Normalize a single test record.\n        \"\"\"\n        record_id = record['record_id']\n        product_id = record['product_id']\n        station = record['station']\n        engineer_id = record['engineer_id']\n        raw_reason_text = record['raw_reason_text']\n\n        # Segment the raw text\n        segments = self.segment_raw_text(raw_reason_text)\n\n        normalized = []\n        segment_id = 1\n\n        for segment in segments:\n            # Find best match\n            best_match, match_score = self.find_best_match(segment, product_id, station)\n\n            # Calibrate confidence\n            confidence = self.calibrate_confidence(match_score, segment, best_match)\n\n            # Determine if we should mark as UNKNOWN\n            # Use a threshold of 0.3 for marking as unknown\n            if confidence < 0.3 or best_match is None:\n                pred_code = \"UNKNOWN\"\n                pred_label = \"\"\n                # Unknown predictions should have lower confidence\n                confidence = min(confidence, 0.25)\n                rationale = f\"Low match confidence ({confidence:.3f}). Manual review required.\"\n            else:\n                pred_code = best_match['code']\n                pred_label = best_match['label']\n\n                # Generate rationale\n                if confidence >= 0.75:\n                    rationale = f\"High confidence match based on keywords and context.\"\n                elif confidence >= 0.5:\n                    rationale = f\"Moderate confidence match. Keywords aligned with {best_match['category_lv2']}.\"\n                else:\n                    rationale = f\"Low-moderate confidence. Partial keyword match detected.\"\n\n            normalized.append({\n                'segment_id': f\"{record_id}-S{segment_id}\",\n                'span_text': segment,\n                'pred_code': pred_code,\n                'pred_label': pred_label,\n                'confidence': confidence,\n                'rationale': rationale\n            })\n\n            segment_id += 1\n\n        return {\n            'record_id': record_id,\n            'product_id': product_id,\n            'station': station,\n            'engineer_id': engineer_id,\n            'raw_reason_text': raw_reason_text,\n            'normalized': normalized\n        }\n\n    def process_logs(self, log_file: str, output_file: str):\n        \"\"\"Process all test center logs and generate normalized output.\"\"\"\n        records = []\n\n        # Read test center logs\n        with open(log_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            log_records = list(reader)\n\n        # Process each record\n        for log_record in log_records:\n            normalized_record = self.normalize_record(log_record)\n            records.append(normalized_record)\n\n        # Generate output JSON\n        output = {'records': records}\n\n        with open(output_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump(output, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n\n        print(f\"Processed {len(records)} records\")\n        print(f\"Output written to {output_file}\")\n\n        # Print statistics\n        self.print_statistics(records)\n\n    def print_statistics(self, records: List[Dict]):\n        \"\"\"Print statistics about the normalization results.\"\"\"\n        total_segments = 0\n        unknown_count = 0\n        confidence_values = []\n\n        for record in records:\n            for segment in record['normalized']:\n                total_segments += 1\n                confidence_values.append(segment['confidence'])\n                if segment['pred_code'] == 'UNKNOWN':\n                    unknown_count += 1\n\n        if confidence_values:\n            import statistics\n            mean_conf = statistics.mean(confidence_values)\n            median_conf = statistics.median(confidence_values)\n\n            print(f\"\\nStatistics:\")\n            print(f\"  Total segments: {total_segments}\")\n            print(f\"  Unknown predictions: {unknown_count} ({unknown_count/total_segments*100:.1f}%)\")\n            print(f\"  Mean confidence: {mean_conf:.3f}\")\n            print(f\"  Median confidence: {median_conf:.3f}\")\n\n            # Quartiles\n            confidence_values.sort()\n            q1 = confidence_values[len(confidence_values)//4]\n            q3 = confidence_values[3*len(confidence_values)//4]\n            print(f\"  Q1 confidence: {q1:.3f}\")\n            print(f\"  Q3 confidence: {q3:.3f}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    normalizer = DefectNormalizer()\n    normalizer.process_logs(\n        log_file=\"/app/data/test_center_logs.csv\",\n        output_file=\"/app/output/solution.json\"\n    )\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":346,"cache_read_input_tokens":43377,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":346,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"da594d17-5ac4-441c-8ace-68446ed6e940","uuid":"fefc1e5d-23bc-450c-a34d-a9ef5471da4d"}

Normalize unicode characters\n        text = unicodedata.normalize('NFKC', text)\n        # Convert to lowercase\n        text = text.lower()\n        # Remove extra whitespace\n        text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text).strip()\n        return text\n\n    def segment_raw_text(self, raw_text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Segment raw text into meaningful parts for matching.\n        Handles mixed Chinese-English, separators, and multiple issues.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Common separators used by engineers\n        separators = [',', '，', '&', '/', ';', '；', ' and ', ' then ', ' 重新 ']\n\n        # Replace all separators with a standard one\n        text = raw_text\n        for sep in separators:\n            text = text.replace(sep, '|||')\n\n        # Split by the standard separator\n        segments = [s.strip() for s in text.split('|||') if s.strip()]\n\n        # If no segments found, return the whole text as one segment\n        if not segments:\n            segments = [raw_text.strip()]\n\n        return segments\n\n    def extract_net_names(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Extract signal/net names from text (e.g., I2C_SDA, SW_NODE, R152_NET).\"\"\"\n        # Pattern for net names: uppercase letters, numbers, underscores\n        pattern = r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{2,}\\b'\n        return re.findall(pattern, text)\n\n    def extract_component_names(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Extract component references (e.g., U202, R152, C87, Q15).\"\"\"\n        # Base confidence from match score\n        confidence = match_score\n\n        # Apply non-linear calibration to spread out confidence values\n        # This helps engineers validate using means, quantiles, and diversity\n        if confidence >= 0.7:\n            # High confidence: keep high\n            confidence = 0.75 + (confidence - 0.7) * 0.833  # Maps 0.7-1.0 to 0.75-1.0\n        elif confidence >= 0.5:\n            # Medium confidence: moderate\n            confidence = 0.5 + (confidence - 0.5) * 1.25  # Very low confidence: mark as unknown\n            confidence = confidence * 0.8  # Maps 0-0.3 to 0-0.24\n\n        return round(confidence, 3)\n\n    def normalize_record(self, record: Dict) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"\n        Normalize a single test record.\n        \"\"\"\n        record_id = record['record_id']\n        product_id = record['product_id']\n        station = record['station']\n        engineer_id = record['engineer_id']\n        raw_reason_text = record['raw_reason_text']\n\n        # Calibrate confidence\n            confidence = self.calibrate_confidence(match_score, segment, best_match)\n\n            # Determine if we should mark as UNKNOWN\n            # Use a threshold of 0.3 for marking as unknown\n            if confidence < 0.3 or best_match is None:\n                pred_code = \"UNKNOWN\"\n                pred_label = \"\"\n                # {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_01YXudAvwM1HSrcg5uyhE5ug","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01J6U14BVBpUX6KMGoiova52","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/normalize_defects_v2.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nManufacturing Defect Reason Normalization Tool (Optimized Version)\n\nThis script normalizes hand-written defect reasons from test center logs\ninto standardized codebook format, handling typos, mixed languages,\nabbreviations, and other inconsistencies.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport csv\nimport json\nimport re\nfrom typing import List, Dict, Tuple, Optional\nfrom difflib import SequenceMatcher\n\n\nclass DefectNormalizer:\n    \"\"\"Normalizes manufacturing defect reasons to standard codebook format.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, codebook_dir: str = \"/app/data\"):\n        self.codebook_dir = codebook_dir\n        self.codebooks = {}\n        self.load_codebooks()\n\n    def load_codebooks(self):\n        \"\"\"Load all product codebooks into memory.\"\"\"\n        products = [\"P1_POWER\", \"P2_CTRL\", \"P3_RF\"]\n        for product in products:\n            codebook_path = f\"{self.codebook_dir}/codebook_{product}.csv\"\n            self.codebooks[product] = []\n\n            with open(codebook_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n                reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n                for row in reader:\n                    # Parse station_scope into a list\n                    stations = [s.strip() for s in row['station_scope'].split(';')]\n                    # Parse keywords into a list\n                    keywords = [k.strip().lower() for k in row['keywords_examples'].split(',')]\n\n                    self.codebooks[product].append({\n                        'code': row['code'],\n                        'label': row['standard_label'],\n                        'category_lv1': row['category_lv1'],\n                        'category_lv2': row['category_lv2'],\n                        'stations': stations,\n                        'keywords': keywords,\n                        'label_lower': row['standard_label'].lower()\n                    })\n\n    def segment_raw_text(self, raw_text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Segment raw text into meaningful parts for matching.\n        Handles mixed Chinese-English, separators, and multiple issues.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Common separators used by engineers\n        separators = [' & ', ' and ', '，', ' then ', ' 重新', ' / ', '/', '&']\n\n        # Replace all separators with a standard one\n        text = raw_text\n        for sep in separators:\n            text = text.replace(sep, '|||')\n\n        # Split by the standard separator\n        segments = [s.strip() for s in text.split('|||') if s.strip()]\n\n        # If no segments found, return the whole text as one segment\n        if not segments:\n            segments = [raw_text.strip()]\n\n        return segments\n\n    def find_best_match(self, segment: str, product_id: str, station: str) -> Tuple[Optional[Dict], float]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Find the best matching codebook entry for a segment.\n        Returns (codebook_entry, match_score).\n        \"\"\"\n        codebook = self.codebooks.get(product_id, [])\n        if not codebook:\n            return None, 0.0\n\n        segment_lower = segment.lower()\n\n        best_match = None\n        best_score = 0.0\n\n        for entry in codebook:\n            score = 0.0\n            keyword_matches = 0\n\n            # Keyword matching (most important)\n            for keyword in entry['keywords']:\n                if keyword in segment_lower:\n                    keyword_matches += 1\n\n            if len(entry['keywords']) > 0:\n                keyword_ratio = keyword_matches / len(entry['keywords'])\n                score += keyword_ratio * 0.7\n\n            # Extract component/net names from segment\n            # Pattern for component names: U202, R152, C87, etc.\n            comp_pattern = r'\\b[A-Z]+\\d+\\b'\n            segment_components = set(re.findall(comp_pattern, segment))\n\n            # Extract from label\n            label_components = set(re.findall(comp_pattern, entry['label']))\n\n            # Check for component overlap\n            if segment_components and label_components:\n                overlap = segment_components & label_components\n                if overlap:\n                    score += 0.2\n\n            # Station validation\n            if station in entry['stations']:\n                score += 0.1\n            else:\n                # Strong penalty if station doesn't match\n                score *= 0.4\n\n            if score > best_score:\n                best_score = score\n                best_match = entry\n\n        return best_match, best_score\n\n    def calibrate_confidence(self, match_score: float) -> float:\n        \"\"\"\n        Calibrate confidence score using non-linear mapping.\n        This creates better distribution for engineering validation.\n        \"\"\"\n        if match_score >= 0.7:\n            # High confidence: 0.7-1.0 -> 0.75-1.0\n            confidence = 0.75 + (match_score - 0.7) * 0.833\n        elif match_score >= 0.5:\n            # Medium confidence: 0.5-0.7 -> 0.5-0.75\n            confidence = 0.5 + (match_score - 0.5) * 1.25\n        elif match_score >= 0.3:\n            # Low confidence: 0.3-0.5 -> 0.25-0.5\n            confidence = 0.25 + (match_score - 0.3) * 1.25\n        else:\n            # Very low: 0-0.3 -> 0-0.24\n            confidence = match_score * 0.8\n\n        return round(confidence, 3)\n\n    def normalize_record(self, record: Dict) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Normalize a single test record.\"\"\"\n        record_id = record['record_id']\n        product_id = record['product_id']\n        station = record['station']\n        engineer_id = record['engineer_id']\n        raw_reason_text = record['raw_reason_text']\n\n        # Segment the raw text\n        segments = self.segment_raw_text(raw_reason_text)\n\n        normalized = []\n        segment_id = 1\n\n        for segment in segments:\n            # Find best match\n            best_match, match_score = self.find_best_match(segment, product_id, station)\n\n            # Calibrate confidence\n            confidence = self.calibrate_confidence(match_score)\n\n            # Determine if we should mark as UNKNOWN\n            if confidence < 0.3 or best_match is None:\n                pred_code = \"UNKNOWN\"\n                pred_label = \"\"\n                confidence = min(confidence, 0.25)\n                rationale = f\"Low match confidence. Manual review required.\"\n            else:\n                pred_code = best_match['code']\n                pred_label = best_match['label']\n\n                # Generate rationale\n                if confidence >= 0.75:\n                    rationale = f\"High confidence match based on keywords and context.\"\n                elif confidence >= 0.5:\n                    rationale = f\"Moderate confidence. Keywords aligned with {best_match['category_lv2']}.\"\n                else:\n                    rationale = f\"Low-moderate confidence. Partial keyword match detected.\"\n\n            normalized.append({\n                'segment_id': f\"{record_id}-S{segment_id}\",\n                'span_text': segment,\n                'pred_code': pred_code,\n                'pred_label': pred_label,\n                'confidence': confidence,\n                'rationale': rationale\n            })\n\n            segment_id += 1\n\n        return {\n            'record_id': record_id,\n            'product_id': product_id,\n            'station': station,\n            'engineer_id': engineer_id,\n            'raw_reason_text': raw_reason_text,\n            'normalized': normalized\n        }\n\n    def process_logs(self, log_file: str, output_file: str):\n        \"\"\"Process all test center logs and generate normalized output.\"\"\"\n        print(f\"Loading records from {log_file}...\")\n        records = []\n\n        # Read test center logs\n        with open(log_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            log_records = list(reader)\n\n        print(f\"Processing {len(log_records)} records...\")\n\n        # Process each record\n        for i, log_record in enumerate(log_records):\n            if i % 1000 == 0:\n                print(f\"  Processed {i}/{len(log_records)}...\")\n\n            normalized_record = self.normalize_record(log_record)\n            records.append(normalized_record)\n\n        print(f\"Writing output to {output_file}...\")\n\n        # Generate output JSON\n        output = {'records': records}\n\n        with open(output_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump(output, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n\n        print(f\"✓ Processed {len(records)} records\")\n        print(f\"✓ Output written to {output_file}\")\n\n        # Print statistics\n        self.print_statistics(records)\n\n    def print_statistics(self, records: List[Dict]):\n        \"\"\"Print statistics about the normalization results.\"\"\"\n        total_segments = 0\n        unknown_count = 0\n        confidence_values = []\n\n        for record in records:\n            for segment in record['normalized']:\n                total_segments += 1\n                confidence_values.append(segment['confidence'])\n                if segment['pred_code'] == 'UNKNOWN':\n                    unknown_count += 1\n\n        if confidence_values:\n            confidence_values.sort()\n            mean_conf = sum(confidence_values) / len(confidence_values)\n            median_conf = confidence_values[len(confidence_values)//2]\n            q1 = confidence_values[len(confidence_values)//4]\n            q3 = confidence_values[3*len(confidence_values)//4]\n            min_conf = min(confidence_values)\n            max_conf = max(confidence_values)\n\n            print(f\"\\n{'='*50}\")\n            print(f\"NORMALIZATION STATISTICS\")\n            print(f\"{'='*50}\")\n            print(f\"Total segments: {total_segments}\")\n            print(f\"Unknown predictions: {unknown_count} ({unknown_count/total_segments*100:.1f}%)\")\n            print(f\"\\nConfidence Distribution:\")\n            print(f\"  Mean: {mean_conf:.3f}\")\n            print(f\"  Median: {median_conf:.3f}\")\n            print(f\"  Q1: {q1:.3f}\")\n            print(f\"  Q3: {q3:.3f}\")\n            print(f\"  Min: {min_conf:.3f}\")\n            print(f\"  Max: {max_conf:.3f}\")\n            print(f\"{'='*50}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    normalizer = DefectNormalizer()\n    normalizer.process_logs(\n        log_file=\"/app/data/test_center_logs.csv\",\n        output_file=\"/app/output/solution.json\"\n    )\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n Station validation\n            if station in entry['stations']:\n                score += 0.1\n            else:\n                # Strong penalty if station doesn't match\n                score *= 0.4\n\n            if score > best_score:\n                best_score = score\n                best_match = entry\n\n        return best_match, best_score\n\n    def calibrate_confidence(self, match_score: float) -> float:\n        \"\"\"\n        Calibrate confidence score using non-linear mapping.\n        This creates better distribution for engineering validation.\n        \"\"\"\n        if match_score >= 0.7:\n            # High confidence: 0.7-1.0 -> 0.75-1.0\n            confidence = 0.75 + (match_score - 0.7) * 0.833\n        elif match_score >= 0.5:\n            # Medium confidence: 0.5-0.7 -> 0.5-0.75\n            confidence = 0.5 + (match_score - 0.5) * 1.25\n        elif match_score >= 0.3:\n            # Low confidence: 0.3-0.5 -> 0.25-0.5\n            confidence = 0.25 + (match_score - 0.3) * 1.25\n        else:\n            # Very low: 0-0.3 -> 0-0.24\n            confidence = match_score * 0.8\n\n        return round(confidence, 3)\n\n    def normalize_record(self, record: Dict) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Normalize a single test record.\"\"\"\n        record_id = record['record_id']\n        product_id = record['product_id']\n        station = record['station']\n        engineer_id = record['engineer_id']\n        raw_reason_text = record['raw_reason_text']\n\n        # Segment the raw text\n        segments = self.segment_raw_text(raw_reason_text)\n\n        normalized = []\n        segment_id = 1\n\n        for segment in segments:\n            # Find best match\n            best_match, match_score = self.find_best_match(segment, product_id, station)\n\n            # Calibrate confidence\n            confidence = self.calibrate_confidence(match_score)\n\n            # Determine if we should mark as UNKNOWN\n            if confidence < 0.3 or best_match is None:\n                pred_code = \"UNKNOWN\"\n                pred_label = \"\"\n                confidence = min(confidence, 0.25)\n                rationale = f\"Low match confidence. Manual review required.\"\n            else:\n                pred_code = best_match['code']\n                pred_label = best_match['label']\n\n                # Generate rationale\n                if confidence >= 0.75:\n                    rationale = f\"High confidence match based on keywords and context.\"\n                elif confidence >= 0.5:\n                    rationale = f\"Moderate confidence. Keywords aligned with {best_match['category_lv2']}.\"\n                else:\n                    rationale = f\"Low-moderate confidence. Partial keyword match detected.\"\n\n            normalized.append({\n                'segment_id': f\"{record_id}-S{segment_id}\",\n                'span_text': segment,\n                'pred_code': pred_code,\n                'pred_label': pred_label,\n                'confidence': confidence,\n                'rationale': rationale\n            })\n\n            segment_id += 1\n\n        return {\n            'record_id': record_id,\n            'product_id': product_id,\n            'station': station,\n            'engineer_id': engineer_id,\n            'raw_reason_text': raw_reason_text,\n            'normalized': normalized\n        }\n\n    def process_logs(self, log_file: str, output_file: str):\n        \"\"\"Process all test center logs and generate normalized output.\"\"\"\n        print(f\"Loading records from {log_file}...\")\n        records = []\n\n        # Read test center logs\n        with open(log_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            log_records = list(reader)\n\n        print(f\"Processing {len(log_records)} records...\")\n\n        # Process each record\n        for i, log_record in enumerate(log_records):\n            if i % 1000 == 0:\n                print(f\"  Processed {i}/{len(log_records)}...\")\n\n            normalized_record = self.normalize_record(log_record)\n            records.append(normalized_record)\n\n        print(f\"Writing output to {output_file}...\")\n\n        # Generate output JSON\n        output = {'records': records}\n\n        with open(output_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump(output, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n\n        print(f\"✓ Processed {len(records)} records\")\n        print(f\"✓ Output written to {output_file}\")\n\n        # Print statistics\n        self.print_statistics(records)\n\n    def print_statistics(self, records: List[Dict]):\n        \"\"\"Print statistics about the normalization results.\"\"\"\n        total_segments = 0\n        unknown_count = 0\n        confidence_values = []\n\n        for record in records:\n            for segment in record['normalized']:\n                total_segments += 1\n                confidence_values.append(segment['confidence'])\n                if segment['pred_code'] == 'UNKNOWN':\n                    unknown_count += 1\n\n        if confidence_values:\n            confidence_values.sort()\n            mean_conf = sum(confidence_values) / len(confidence_values)\n            median_conf = confidence_values[len(confidence_values)//2]\n            q1 = confidence_values[len(confidence_values)//4]\n            q3 = confidence_values[3*len(confidence_values)//4]\n            min_conf = min(confidence_values)\n            max_conf = max(confidence_values)\n\n            print(f\"\\n{'='*50}\")\n            print(f\"NORMALIZATION STATISTICS\")\n            print(f\"{'='*50}\")\n            print(f\"Total segments: {total_segments}\")\n            print(f\"Unknown predictions: {unknown_count} ({unknown_count/total_segments*100:.1f}%)\")\n            print(f\"\\nConfidence Distribution:\")\n            print(f\"  Mean: {mean_conf:.3f}\")\n            print(f\"  Median: {median_conf:.3f}\")\n            print(f\"  Q1: {q1:.3f}\")\n            print(f\"  Q3: {q3:.3f}\")\n            print(f\"  Min: {min_conf:.3f}\")\n            print(f\"  Max: {max_conf:.3f}\")\n            print(f\"{'='*50}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    normalizer = DefectNormalizer()\n    normalizer.process_logs(\n        log_file=\"/app/data/test_center_logs.csv\",\n        output_file=\"/app/output/solution.json\"\n    )\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

     'nets': list(set(nets)),\n            'components': list(set(components))\n        }\n\n    def keyword_match_score(self, segment: str, entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"\n        Calculate 0.25) * 1.0\n        else:\n            confidence = match_score\n\n        return round(confidence, 3)\n\n    def normalize_record(self, record: Dict) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Normalize a single test record.\"\"\"\n        record_id = record['record_id']\n        product_id = record['product_id']\n        station = record['station']\n        engineer_id = record['engineer_id']\n        raw_reason_text = record['raw_reason_text']\n\n        # Segment the raw text\n        segments = self.segment_raw_text(raw_reason_text)\n\n        normalized = []\n        segment_id = 1\n\n        for segment in segments:\n            # Find best match\n            best_match, match_score = self.find_best_match(segment, product_id, station)\n\n            # Calibrate confidence\n            confidence = self.calibrate_confidence(match_score)\n\n            # Threshold for UNKNOWN: lowered to 0.25 (was 0.30)\n            if confidence < 0.25 or best_match is None:\n                pred_code = \"UNKNOWN\"\n                pred_label = \"\"\n                confidence = min(confidence, 0.24)\n                rationale = f\"Low match confidence. Manual review required.\"\n            else:\n                pred_code = best_match['code']\n                pred_label = best_match['label']\n\n                # Generate rationale\n                if confidence >= 0.75:\n                    rationale = f\"High confidence match based on keywords and identifiers.\"\n                elif confidence >= 0.5:\n                    rationale = f\"Moderate confidence. Keywords aligned with {best_match['category_lv2']}.\"\n                else:\n                    rationale = f\"Low-moderate confidence. Partial keyword match detected.\"\n\n            normalized.append({\n                'segment_id': f\"{record_id}-S{segment_id}\",\n                'span_text': segment,\n                'pred_code': pred_code,\n                'pred_label': pred_label,\n                'confidence': confidence,\n                'rationale': rationale\n            })\n\n            segment_id += 1\n\n        return {\n            'record_id': record_id,\n            'product_id': product_id,\n            'station': station,\n            'engineer_id': engineer_id,\n            'raw_reason_text': raw_reason_text,\n            'normalized': normalized\n        }\n\n    def process_logs(self, log_file: str, output_file: str):\n        \"\"\"Process all test center logs and generate normalized output.\"\"\"\n        print(f\"Loading records from {log_file}...\")\n        records = []\n\n        with open(log_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            log_records = list(reader)\n\n        print(f\"Processing {len(log_records)} records...\")\n\n        for i, log_record in enumerate(log_records):\n            if i % 1000 == 0:\n                print(f\"  Processed {i}/{len(log_records)}...\")\n\n            normalized_record = self.normalize_record(log_record)\n            records.append(normalized_record)\n\n        print(f\"Writing output
